# Live Progress Log (Real-Time Update Required)

Purpose: this file is the append-only execution log for ongoing development.
Every model/operator must update this file after meaningful actions.

---

## 1) Update Rules (Mandatory)

1. Append only. Do not rewrite old records unless they are factually wrong.
2. Use local time (Asia/Shanghai), ISO format: `YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS +08:00`.
3. One entry per meaningful action batch (code edit, experiment run, diagnosis, decision).
4. Any major modification must be logged in this file before handing off:
   - algorithm changes
   - training config changes
   - scripts/tooling behavior changes
   - experiment conclusions that alter next-step strategy
5. Each entry must include:
   - Task ID / short title
   - Hypothesis or objective
   - What was changed
   - Evidence (command, logs, metrics, or file diff summary)
   - Decision taken
   - Next step
   - Suggested commit message (`[EXP_ID] one-line intent`)
6. If blocked, explicitly mark `BLOCKED` or `BLOCKED_WAIT_REMOTE` and state blocker + owner.
7. Never hide failures. Failed experiments are first-class records.
8. If one `EXP_ID` has multiple entries, the latest entry status is authoritative for triage.

---

## 2) Current Snapshot

- Project phase: Mask diagnosis -> supervision-strengthening design
- Main unresolved problem: mask is high-entropy / too flat under current indirect supervision
- Last known conclusion: tower mismatch is not the dominant root cause
- Primary reference doc: `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/docs/algorithm1/handoff/mask_diagnosis_full_history.md`

---

## 3) Entry Template

Copy this block for each new entry:

```md
## [YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS +08:00] <Task ID / Title>

- Owner: <model/operator>
- Status: IN_PROGRESS | DONE | BLOCKED | BLOCKED_WAIT_REMOTE | ROLLED_BACK
- Objective:
  - ...
- Changes:
  - Files:
    - `absolute/path/a`
    - `absolute/path/b`
  - Code/Config summary:
    - ...
- Evidence:
  - Commands:
    - `...`
  - Key outputs/metrics:
    - ...
- Decision:
  - ...
- Risks/Notes:
  - ...
- Next step:
  - ...
- Commit message:
  - `[<EXP_ID>] <one-line intent>`
```

---

## 4) Live Entries

## [2026-03-01 17:26:15 +08:00] ALG1-MASK-20260301-001-OC Implement directed self-attn + cross-attn soft-mask path (no-regression default)

- Owner: OC
- Status: IN_PROGRESS
- Objective:
  - Test whether a directed self-attention refinement stacked on language-to-token cross-attention can improve mask selectivity while keeping baseline behavior unchanged by default.
- Changes:
  - Files:
    - `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/starVLA/model/framework/MapAnythingLlava3DPI.py`
    - `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/starVLA/model/modules/action_model/LayerwiseFM_ActionHeader.py`
  - Code/Config summary:
    - Added new soft-mask generator mode switch: `soft_mask_generator` in {`similarity`, `directed_self_cross`}.
    - Added directed refinement controls: `soft_mask_directed_mix`, `soft_mask_directed_causal`, `soft_mask_directed_self_scale`.
    - Implemented directed path in `_build_soft_mask`: language->token cross-attn (base) + token-token directed self-attn contextualization + second cross-attn refinement + weighted fusion.
    - Kept default mode `similarity` to preserve existing behavior unless explicitly enabled.
    - Added diagnostics for new path (`soft_mask_directed_*` metrics).
- Evidence:
  - Commands:
    - `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/tools/handoff/bootstrap_session.sh start --module MASK --owner OC --title "Investigate directed attention mask generator design"`
    - `python -m py_compile /Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/starVLA/model/framework/MapAnythingLlava3DPI.py /Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/starVLA/model/modules/action_model/LayerwiseFM_ActionHeader.py`
    - `python - <<'PY' ... import torch ...` (failed in current shell env)
  - Key outputs/metrics:
    - EXP_ID created: `ALG1-MASK-20260301-001-OC`.
    - Syntax check passed for both modified files.
    - Runtime dry test blocked because current `python` environment lacks `torch` (`ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'torch'`).
- Decision:
  - Keep implementation merged as opt-in path and move to controlled runtime validation in training/diagnostic environment with PyTorch installed.
- Risks/Notes:
  - Directed attention path does not mathematically guarantee breaking exact-uniform mask if upstream query-token signals remain fully uniform; objective/supervision strength still remains key.
  - Current validation is compile-level only; metric-level effect still pending.
- Next step:
  - Run A/B diagnostics (`similarity` vs `directed_self_cross`) on 10-100 samples and compare required mask metrics + action-side contrast terms before enabling in training runs.

      - `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/_remote_runs/1229_libero4in1_MapAnythingLlava3DPI_s42_20260301_133009__ALG1-MASK-20260301-002-OC/metrics.jsonl`
      - `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/_remote_runs/1229_libero4in1_MapAnythingLlava3DPI_s42_20260301_133009__ALG1-MASK-20260301-002-OC/train.raw.log`

  - Effective config confirms teacher branch is enabled:

    - `soft_mask_teacher_confidence_floor: 0.02`

  - Reliability script output agrees (post100 window):

    - FAIL: confidence / alpha_cos / negative-control separation

    - `debug/health/has_nonfinite=0`
    - `debug/action_health/has_nonfinite=0`
    - feedback/task grad norms are non-zero.

- Next step:

    - much lower floor (e.g., `0.002`) to match observed confidence scale.
  - Keep weight at 0.02 first, verify `teacher_kl>0` and `soft_mask_teacher_loss_weighted>0` by step>=120.
- Commit message:
  - `[ALG1-MASK-20260301-002-OC] diagnose m_clip gating-off due high confidence floor in first teacher-enabled run`

## [2026-03-01 23:14:19 +08:00] ALG1-MASK-20260301-003-OC Systematic low-selectivity code audit

- Owner: OC
- Status: DONE
- Objective:
  - Systematically audit MASK pipeline code for implementation-level causes of persistent low selectivity.
  - Validate whether low selectivity is mainly objective weakness or also affected by a concrete code-path scaling issue.
- Changes:
  - Files:
    - `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/starVLA/model/framework/MapAnythingLlava3DPI.py`
    - `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/docs/algorithm1/handoff/progress_live.md`
  - Code/Config summary:
    - Audited soft-mask build path, teacher supervision path, and feedback contrast path.
    - Identified and fixed a high-probability implementation issue in `_build_soft_mask`: for `soft_mask_score_norm=l2_only`, normalization was applied on full hidden vectors before head split, which shrinks each head norm and flattens logits.
    - Changed L2 normalization to operate per-head after head split (`qh/vh/gh`), preserving cosine scale at head level.
- Evidence:
  - Commands:
    - `rg -n "def _build_soft_mask|soft_mask_teacher|feedback_mask_contrast|soft_mask_score_norm|soft_mask_generator" /Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/starVLA/model/framework/MapAnythingLlava3DPI.py`
    - `sed -n '927,1325p' /Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/starVLA/model/framework/MapAnythingLlava3DPI.py`
    - `python - <<'PY' ...` (metrics parsing on run `..._20260301_135730__ALG1-MASK-20260301-002-OC` for logits/entropy/topk diagnostics)
    - `python - <<'PY' ...` (synthetic check: full-vector L2 then split vs per-head L2, observed logits std ratio ≈ `4.0002` with `num_heads=4`)
    - `python -m py_compile /Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/starVLA/model/framework/MapAnythingLlava3DPI.py`
  - Key outputs/metrics:
    - Runtime diagnostics (post step>=100) show soft-mask remains close to uniform:
      - `soft_mask_token_num=512`
      - `soft_mask_logits_std_vis≈0.0407`
      - `soft_mask_attn_top1_mean_vis≈0.00232` (uniform baseline `1/512≈0.00195`)
      - `soft_mask_entropy≈6.2368` vs `ln(512)=6.2383`
    - Directed refinement impact remains very small:
      - `soft_mask_directed_refine_delta_vis≈9.7e-05`
    - Synthetic normalization test result:
      - global-L2-then-split logits std `0.00782`
      - per-head-L2 logits std `0.03128`
      - ratio `~4.0` (matches configured head count), confirming scale shrink from pre-split normalization.
- Decision:
  - Confirmed this is a concrete code-path issue that can suppress selectivity regardless of downstream supervision tweaks.
  - Applied minimal fix first (per-head L2 normalization) before further objective tuning.
- Risks/Notes:
  - This fix changes soft-mask logit scale distribution; next remote run is required to validate whether selectivity metrics improve and whether action loss remains stable.
  - Additional non-patched risk remains: `token_n=min(Nv,Ng)` prefix truncation may still cause cross-modal token alignment loss when modality token layouts differ.
- Next step:
  - Launch an A/B remote run using the same config except this patch, then compare:
    - `soft_mask_logits_std_vis`, `soft_mask_attn_top1_mean_vis`
    - `soft_mask_entropy`, `soft_mask_topk_mass_32`, `soft_mask_alpha_max_mean`
    - `feedback_mask_contrast_*`, `delta_action_*`, `action_dit_loss`
  - If selectivity still flat after this fix, prioritize token alignment handling between vision/geometric token sequences and then revisit teacher target sharpening.
- Commit message:
  - `[ALG1-MASK-20260301-003-OC] fix soft-mask l2 normalization to operate per head`

## [2026-03-02 06:40:36 +08:00] ALG1-MASK-20260301-003-OC Token-length alignment fix for `token_n=min(Nv,Ng)` path

- Owner: OC
- Status: DONE
- Objective:
  - Address the known risk that `token_n=min(Nv,Ng)` followed by prefix truncation can destroy cross-modal correspondence and weaken selectivity transfer.
- Planned approach (implemented in this entry):
  - Replace prefix truncation with range-preserving resampling alignment so selected tokens cover the full source sequence instead of only the prefix.
  - Apply the same alignment rule in both soft-mask builder and residual `token_delta_geo` branch to keep behavior consistent.
  - Add diagnostics for source token lengths, truncation amount, and whether resampling actually happened.
- Changes:
  - Files:
    - `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/starVLA/model/framework/MapAnythingLlava3DPI.py`
    - `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/docs/algorithm1/handoff/progress_live.md`
  - Code summary:
    - Added helper: `_resample_tokens_for_alignment(tokens, target_len)`.
      - `target_len < src_len`: evenly-spaced index sampling across full range `[0, src_len-1]`.
      - `target_len == src_len`: no-op.
      - Includes safe fallback for degenerate/rare cases.
    - Updated `_build_soft_mask(...)`:
      - Replaced direct prefix slice with helper-based alignment.
      - Added diagnostics:
        - `soft_mask_token_num_vis_src`, `soft_mask_token_num_geo_src`, `soft_mask_token_num_mismatch`
        - `soft_mask_token_truncated_vis`, `soft_mask_token_truncated_geo`
        - `soft_mask_token_align_resampled_vis`, `soft_mask_token_align_resampled_geo`
    - Updated Path-A residual token-delta branch:
      - Replaced prefix slicing for `geometric_tokens/geometric_tokens_next/vision_tokens` with helper-based alignment to shared `token_n`.
      - Added diagnostics:
        - `residual_token_num_geo_src`, `residual_token_num_geo_next_src`, `residual_token_num_vis_src`
        - `residual_token_truncated_geo`, `residual_token_truncated_geo_next`, `residual_token_truncated_vis`
        - `residual_token_align_resampled_geo`, `residual_token_align_resampled_geo_next`, `residual_token_align_resampled_vis`
        - `residual_token_align_range_resample`
- Evidence:
  - Syntax check passed:
    - `python -m py_compile /Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/starVLA/model/framework/MapAnythingLlava3DPI.py`
  - `git diff` confirms both target regions updated:
    - soft-mask construction path (`_build_soft_mask`)
    - residual token-delta path (`token_delta_geo`)
- Decision:
  - Keep this alignment fix and validate via next remote run; expected effect is reduced prefix bias when modal token lengths differ.
- Risks/Notes:
  - This is a structural alignment change; final effectiveness must be judged by remote metrics/logs (especially selectivity + action loss + teacher diagnostics).
- Next step:
  - Launch/continue MASK run and monitor newly added alignment diagnostics together with:
    - `soft_mask_entropy`, `soft_mask_topk_mass_32`, `soft_mask_alpha_max_mean_vis`
    - `action_dit_loss`, `delta_action_clip_saturation_frac`
    - teacher reliability (`teacher_kl`, `teacher_alpha_cos`, `teacher_confidence_mean`, `teacher_neg_top1_gap`)
- Commit message:
  - `[ALG1-MASK-20260301-003-OC] replace prefix truncation with range-preserving token alignment for soft-mask and residual paths`

## [2026-03-02 19:37:13 +08:00] ALG1-INFRA-20260302-003-OC Complete system audit of training-eval and eval_libero pipeline

- Owner: OC
- Status: DONE
- Objective:
  - Systematically audit current eval stack (trainer internal eval + LIBERO rollout eval) and identify infra/system causes for poor real-world eval outcomes.
- Changes:
  - Files:
    - `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/docs/algorithm1/handoff/progress_live.md`
  - Code/Config summary:
    - No runtime code patch in this step.
    - Produced code-level and runtime-level diagnosis for eval reliability and train/eval mismatch.
- Evidence:
  - Commands:
    - `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/tools/handoff/bootstrap_session.sh start --module EVAL --owner OC --title "Audit eval and eval_libero reliability end-to-end"` (failed: invalid module enum)
    - `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/tools/handoff/bootstrap_session.sh start --module INFRA --owner OC --title "System audit of eval and eval_libero reliability"`
    - `nl -ba /Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/starVLA/training/train_starvla.py | sed -n '700,1095p'`
    - `nl -ba /Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/starVLA/model/framework/MapAnythingLlava3DPI.py | sed -n '2740,2795p'`
    - `nl -ba /Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/starVLA/model/framework/MapAnythingLlava3DPI.py | sed -n '3336,3542p'`
    - `nl -ba /Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/examples/LIBERO/eval_files/model2libero_interface.py | sed -n '56,210p'`
    - `nl -ba /Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/examples/LIBERO/eval_files/eval_libero.py | sed -n '206,380p'`
    - `python - <<'PY' ...` summary over `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/_remote_runs/rt_metrics.jsonl`
    - `python - <<'PY' ...` parse `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/_remote_runs/eval_libero.log` success lines
  - Key outputs/metrics:
    - EXP_ID generated: `ALG1-INFRA-20260302-003-OC` (module `EVAL` not accepted by helper; mapped to `INFRA`).
    - Trainer internal eval uses a fixed batch sampled from `vla_train_dataloader` (`_init_fixed_eval_batch`), not a held-out eval dataset.
    - Trainer eval metric is normalized-action MSE on offline samples; does not measure LIBERO task success.
    - `eval_libero` default executes chunked open-loop with `action_chunk_size = future_action_window_size + 1` (current observed chunk size = 8).
    - Runtime metrics (`rt_metrics.jsonl`) show chunk refresh every 8 steps; policy latency mean:
      - refresh steps: ~`912.29ms`
      - non-refresh steps: ~`3.86ms`
    - Parsed eval log snapshot (`/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/_remote_runs/eval_libero.log`) shows `1/12` success lines (`8.33%`) in captured window.
- Decision:
  - Current poor eval is likely multi-factor and strongly related to evaluation-stack design mismatch, not just model quality:
    - train-time eval proxy is weakly correlated with LIBERO success,
    - rollout-side chunked execution and stateful Path-A coupling create sensitivity,
    - eval infra lacks strict server/client run-identity consistency checks.
- Risks/Notes:
  - `repeat_infer_debug_times > 1` can issue repeated server inference on the same step and mutate policy-side rolling memories during debugging.
  - `MapAnythingLlava3DPI.forward` increments `_soft_mask_teacher_forward_step` regardless of train/eval mode; teacher-forced eval ablation may shift teacher-step bookkeeping.
  - `eval_libero` and `run_policy_server` can be pointed to different checkpoints if CKPT is not strictly unified; currently no handshake guard.
- Next step:
  - Implement P0 eval hardening patch set:
    1) add explicit server/client run-id consistency check,
    2) add rollout-mode switch (`full_chunk` vs `receding_horizon`) with required Path-A compatibility guard,
    3) add eval preset profiles (deterministic + debug-safe),
    4) add held-out offline eval dataloader path in trainer to separate train-proxy from rollout KPI.
- Commit message:
  - `[ALG1-INFRA-20260302-003-OC] audit eval stack and isolate train-vs-rollout mismatch risks`

## [2026-03-02 19:47:19 +08:00] ALG1-INFRA-20260302-003-OC Enable eval_libero receding-horizon mode and Path-A residual observability

- Owner: OC
- Status: DONE
- Objective:
  - Make eval_libero capable of per-step replanning (for Path-A geometric residual effectiveness) and expose residual-mode debug evidence in runtime metrics.
- Changes:
  - Files:
    - `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/examples/LIBERO/eval_files/eval_libero.py`
    - `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/examples/LIBERO/eval_files/model2libero_interface.py`
    - `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/docs/algorithm1/handoff/progress_live.md`
  - Code/Config summary:
    - Added `eval_control_mode` (`chunk` | `receding_horizon`) to eval entrypoint.
    - In `receding_horizon` mode, force per-step policy refresh and first-action execution (`action_ref_index=0`) to maximize Path-A update frequency.
    - Added guard to skip repeated inference debug calls on refresh steps to avoid mutating policy-side rolling memories.
    - Extended `rt_metrics` logging with Path-A residual-mode fields:
      - `residual_mode_*`, `residual_mode_used_*`, `token_delta_geo_*`, `residual_token_num`, `delta_geo_token_norm_mean`.
- Evidence:
  - Commands:
    - `git diff -- examples/LIBERO/eval_files/model2libero_interface.py examples/LIBERO/eval_files/eval_libero.py`
    - `python -m py_compile /Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/examples/LIBERO/eval_files/model2libero_interface.py /Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla/examples/LIBERO/eval_files/eval_libero.py`
  - Key outputs/metrics:
    - Python syntax check passed for both modified eval files.
- Decision:
  - Keep default behavior backward-compatible (`eval_control_mode=chunk`), and provide explicit diagnostic mode (`receding_horizon`) for geometric residual verification.
- Risks/Notes:
  - `receding_horizon` increases policy calls (higher latency/cost); recommended for diagnosis and Path-A validation runs.
  - Path-A residual evidence still requires `request_policy_debug_info=True` so server debug fields are returned.
- Next step:
  - Run A/B on same checkpoint:
    1) `eval_control_mode=chunk`
    2) `eval_control_mode=receding_horizon`, `request_policy_debug_info=true`
    and compare `residual_mode_used_token_delta_geo`, success rate, and latency.
- Commit message:
  - `[ALG1-INFRA-20260302-003-OC] add receding-horizon eval mode and path-a residual debug metrics`

  - Code/Config summary:
    - Added `ensure_repo_context.sh` as a unified guard for repo root identity and optional VLM scope (`qwen_only|mixed|auto`).
    - Guard requires explicit expected root when called with `--require-expected-root`, and fails fast on mismatch or missing root.
    - `pre_dev_readiness.sh`, `preflight.sh`, and `bootstrap_session.sh start` now call repo guard before allowing normal startup flow.
    - Prompt/checklist/skill/thread docs now require explicit `STARVLA_EXPECTED_REPO_ROOT` and guard command before work starts.
    - Core docs replaced machine-local absolute paths with `<REPO_ROOT>` placeholders to avoid path carry-over mistakes across environments.

    - `STARVLA_EXPECTED_REPO_ROOT=/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla-v2 STARVLA_EXPECTED_VLM_SCOPE=mixed bash tools/handoff/ensure_repo_context.sh --require-expected-root`
    - `STARVLA_EXPECTED_REPO_ROOT=/tmp/not-this-repo STARVLA_EXPECTED_VLM_SCOPE=mixed bash tools/handoff/ensure_repo_context.sh --require-expected-root`
    - `STARVLA_EXPECTED_REPO_ROOT=/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla-v2 STARVLA_EXPECTED_VLM_SCOPE=qwen_only bash tools/handoff/ensure_repo_context.sh --require-expected-root`
    - `STARVLA_EXPECTED_REPO_ROOT=/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla-v2 STARVLA_EXPECTED_VLM_SCOPE=mixed bash tools/handoff/pre_dev_readiness.sh`
    - `bash tools/handoff/bootstrap_session.sh start --module INFRA --owner OC --title "guard check should fail"`
    - `rg -n "/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla-v2" docs/starvla_retrofit/handoff docs/starvla_retrofit/skills/starvla-retrofit-ops -g '*.md' -g '!**/ALG1-INFRA-*'`
  - Key outputs/metrics:
    - Guard pass example: `REPO_CONTEXT_OK=YES` when expected root matches current repo.
    - Guard hard fail example: `expected root path not found` + `REPO_CONTEXT_OK=NO` (wrong target repo).
    - Guard hard fail example: `qwen_only scope violated` with offending runtime file references listed.
    - `bootstrap_session.sh start` now fails immediately when expected root is unset (prevents unpinned session creation).
    - `pre_dev_readiness.sh` now surfaces `expected_repo` and runs guard as first gate.
- Decision:
  - Repo-identity pinning is now enforced at all startup choke points, and prompt/skill templates now force explicit target-root declaration before any coding action.
- Risks/Notes:
  - This repo still contains historical MapAnything/LLaVA3D runtime paths; therefore `STARVLA_EXPECTED_VLM_SCOPE=qwen_only` will intentionally fail here.
  - For Qwen-only server repo, use `STARVLA_EXPECTED_VLM_SCOPE=qwen_only`; for mixed legacy repo auditing, use `mixed`.
- Next step:
  - Mirror the same guard script + startup wiring to `/2025233147/zzq/SpatialVLA_llava3d/starvla_test_qwen/starVLA`, then run one full startup dry-run in that repo with `qwen_only`.
- Commit message:
  - `[ALG1-INFRA-20260405-002-OC] add repo-context hard guard across dev/prompt/skill system`

    - `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla-v2/starVLA/model/framework/QwenPI.py`

    - Added `optional_loss_utils.build_optional_hook_targets(...)` to parse sample-level `pseudo_labels` (`trigger_label`, `risk_score`, `delta_action_norm`, `correction_mask`, `affected_region_prior`) into masked tensors.
    - Replaced `a_loss/corrective_loss` zero stubs in `QwenPI` and `QwenGR00T` with real auxiliary heads and losses:

      - matches records by `(dataset_name, trajectory_id, sample_step)` and attaches `pseudo_labels` / `remaining_chunk` onto runtime samples.
      - supports optional `correction_supervision_filter_to_index` to sample only matched correction steps during smoke verification.
    - Extended `starvla_train_pi.yaml` with new optional data/hook weights (default off / backward-compatible).

## [2026-04-07 07:51:18 +08:00] ALG1-INFRA-20260407-001-OC add standalone A-module prediction interface (lite/standalone switch)

- Owner: OC
- Status: IN_PROGRESS
- Objective:
  - Keep current P3/P4-lite path stable while introducing a clean interface for future standalone A-module integration.
- Changes:
  - Files:
    - `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla-v2/starVLA/model/framework/a_module_interface.py`
    - `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla-v2/starVLA/model/framework/QwenPI.py`
    - `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla-v2/starVLA/model/framework/QwenGR00T.py`
    - `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla-v2/starVLA/config/training/starvla_train_pi.yaml`
    - `/Users/bazinga/code/my-starvla-v2/docs/algorithm1/handoff/progress_live.md`
  - Code/Config summary:
    - Added `a_module_interface.py` with interface factory:
      - `mode=lite`: current in-graph heads (`a_risk_head/a_trigger_head/corrective_*`) behavior.
      - `mode=standalone`: read external predictions from sample payload (`a_outputs` by default), with optional fallback to lite heads.
    - Refactored `QwenPI` / `QwenGR00T` optional-hook computation to consume interface outputs instead of hard-coding lite head calls.
    - Added config block:
      - `framework.a_module.mode: lite|standalone`
      - `framework.a_module.standalone.{input_key, allow_pseudo_labels_fallback, strict_missing, fallback_to_lite}`
    - Preserved checkpoint key compatibility for existing lite heads by keeping original module field names unchanged.
- Evidence:
  - Commands:
    - `python3 -m py_compile starVLA/model/framework/a_module_interface.py starVLA/model/framework/QwenPI.py starVLA/model/framework/QwenGR00T.py`
  - Key outputs/metrics:
    - Python compile check passed for all modified framework files.
- Decision:
  - Default remains `lite`; new interface is opt-in and backward-compatible for current P3/P4-lite runs.
- Risks/Notes:
  - `standalone` mode currently defines protocol + ingestion path only; external A-module model wiring/training loop is next.
  - `allow_pseudo_labels_fallback` is disabled by default to avoid label leakage from training targets.
- Next step:
  - Add smoke command set for `framework.a_module.mode=standalone` (with and without fallback), then scaffold external A-model contract implementation.
- Commit message:
  - `[ALG1-INFRA-20260407-001-OC] add standalone A-module interface switch for optional-hook path`
